# Active build checkpoint — 7 October 2026

## Latest continuation — 21 October 2026: native script HTTPS transport

Continued without approval questions. Added scriptHTTP poll, artifact retrieval, attended/unattended final claim, historical result receipt and private output upload methods, plus cancellation validation against a durable original dispatch. Calls use fixed same-origin routes, the current agent credential, bounded bodies, three-second request contexts, explicit identity encoding, strict response media types and closed original-byte JSON/signature validation. Per-call HTTP clients reject redirects and omit cookie jars even if shared client settings change. Upstream errors retain only safe status codes. No private output or upstream body enters error strings.

Artifact retrieval checks the exact signed byte count/hash/UTF-8 and rechecks dispatch expiry before returning bytes. Claims derive their fields from the verified dispatch, enforce explicit attended consent or its absence for unattended mode, and validate the returned lease at response time. Results and output can recover after dispatch expiry using the original verified command; historical acknowledgements are not new launch grants or proof that content remains available. Poll cancellation envelopes are validated for current agent/type/time, then must be bound to the recorded original command/attempt/hash before application. Returned artifact bytes still require protected staging; this transport never starts or writes a script.

Final Go B passes **24 top-level tests/177 subtests**, no skips, with18 source pins and seven original PHP/native inputs unchanged. Local TLS listeners replay original PHP responses and check the actual submitted claims, results, output headers and bytes. Positive checks cover attended/unattended flows, ten original output cases, lost-response retries and renewed cancellation after dispatch expiry. Denials cover changed artifacts, bad signatures, mismatched receipts, foreign agents, expired/in-flight leases, redirects/cookies, malformed/oversized/compressed replies, private error redaction and cancellation of an active request. Separate shared-client origin/privacy/JSON tests pass **3/0**, with23 total source/dependency pins frozen. Vet and Windows ARM64 compilation pass; the PE machine is independently checked as0xaa64 and ARM64 was not executed. All ten recorded Go/Node runners are terminal.

Earlier Go A22/171 is preserved; only the HTTP test file changed afterward to add negative and in-flight cases. A runner reference typo prevented the first B startup before any child or tests ran; it is preserved as unaccepted evidence. The B ARM64 check's recorded label contains a typo; its actual arguments, output path and independently inspected PE machine establish ARM64 compilation. All96 preceding server/source fixture pins and all16 preceding Go pins remain unchanged, so the previous private-output server evidence is retained without another database run. Fresh native collector output remains byte-identical. Evidence: .endpoint-tools/script-native-transport-20261021/final-check.json.

This is local TLS interoperability, not an installed agent talking to a deployed PHP service. The transport is not wired into the agent loop, does not advertise script capability and leaves production execution/ordinary queue closed. Next is the durable native journal and protected staging/output recovery, followed by runtime provisioning, real desktop consent, owned-job executor/controller integration and cancellation/quiescence. Concurrent-writer, UI/browser/native UI, installed/signing/hardware, operating and complete customer release acceptance remain OPEN. Full goal ACTIVE.


## Latest continuation — 21 October 2026: private script output and fixed retention

Continued without approval questions. Added encrypted output storage bound to the existing canonical delivery and accepted immutable result. Credential-only uploads preserve exact bounded UTF-8 bytes and reject changed hashes, size, truncation, identities or unsupported input. Staff receipt/content reads require current account/MFA, approved Site/device scope, requester ownership or command administration, and the separate scriptOutputView grant; existing run/admin privileges do not substitute for it. Content reads are audited without raw text and use private plain-text attachment responses. Permission registration grants no role access.

Retention is anchored to the original result receipt and signed manifest duration, so delayed uploads and retries cannot extend it. Expiry erases ciphertext while retaining the immutable acknowledgement. Reads, replay and the independent bounded scheduled purge remove expired content; expired content reads commit erasure before returning410. A replay can confirm historical acceptance without restoring data. Native pure upload/receipt validators bind exact content and accept historical receipts after expiry without claiming current availability or process termination.

## Authoritative current decisions
Read OBLI-DECISIONS.md. All earlier AI questions were answered.
- Windows-only computer agents; Windows/Linux site collectors.
- One Windows installer with Computer support / Site monitoring / Both roles, separate service/UI privileges internally.
- Obli Orbit (02) is the selected design, refined per the user's feedback and animated with motion/reduced-motion controls.
- Staff AI read-only; central OpenAI/Claude settings/keys; private chat and selected ticket sharing.
- Technician full remote screen/control and approved actions, central configurable consent/unattended policies, default user clicks Allow connection, active notice/Stop session.
- IT & Support > Remote support plus ticket linked-computer shortcut.
- Multiple customer organisations mean independent single-tenant deployments (working interpretation explained to the user); installer must carry that deployment's server/enrolment configuration. No cross-tenant SaaS layer.

### Delegated backend review integrated — disabled-service repair

GPT-6.1 Sol backend review identified a concrete Windows repair defect: a disabled owned service was started before automatic startup was restored, so Windows would refuse StartService. The correction gives repair startup-restoration-before-start ordering while retaining fresh installation's readiness-before-automatic-start behavior. Startup mutation re-reads ownership. Healthy running services are not stopped or restarted; foreign/cancelled services are not mutated. Public review/receipt contracts and the graphical flow remain unchanged.

Parent reviewed all three changed Go files: internal/setup/service_windows.go, repair_windows.go and repair_start_windows_test.go. Focused tests/vet e44aae FINISHED exit 0 (Test(ServiceRepair|FreshInstallation|RepairReview), count=1), including failure/retained-binding/separate retry records and changed ownership during fresh-install readiness. Earlier regression 56d948 correctly failed before the ownership recheck correction. Windows ARM64 test compilation a4999b FINISHED exit 0. All this agent's calls are terminal, with no live process handles. Tests use injected service operations; actual signed installed SCM repair remains an acceptance gate. No new C#/XAML change required, so the already-passing native rendering/contract checks were retained.


### Delegated backend implementation integrated — collector recovery retention

GPT-6.1 Sol implemented CollectorEnrollmentRecoveryPruner, a bounded monitoring:prune-enrolment-recovery command and five-minute non-overlapping schedule. Only expired recovery_response and recovery_identity_fingerprint values are cleared; consumed replies stay recoverable until expiry. Collector credentials/identity, history, token hash and current-authority rules remain intact. The fixed UTC cutoff is rechecked in the write so an expiry extension after selection preserves the sealed reply.

Parent reviewed the service, command, exact schedule addition and regression tests. Original isolated feature-test session 74098 FINISHED exit 0: 18 passed, 104 assertions, 366.58 seconds. It was polled without restart during the known slow schema bootstrap. Pint and PHP syntax checks passed. Tests cover loss/replay before expiry, exact-expiry cleanup, preserved enrolment/collector fields, bounded batches/orphaned fingerprints and a deterministic selection-to-write expiry-extension race. The latter is not a separate-connection lock-contention witness. See collector-recovery-retention.md. No production migration or real-data deletion.

Both delegated agents have finished with no live test/build handles. Parent documentation and whitespace reconciliation is the final step for this increment. Start durably reserves immutable actor/browser/request/source/support context and shared peer capacity before external governed credential retrieval. The real CredentialLeaseProvider runs outside domain transaction locks, with the separate cctv:hikvision:live:read_only capability. Its registered current reference/version/Site/capability grant bounds the final session and TURN absolute expiry. Private material and source context are encrypted and hidden. Pending or failed preparation cannot reissue through the same request key; scope changes during retrieval release the obtained lease and retain the failure. The same Start key cannot be recovered from another browser. Prepared credential identity and session/ICE deadlines cannot change.

Renewal receipts retain the original expected generation and cannot extend the deadline twice after a lost response. Original source/TURN/offer bytes and absolute lifetime remain fixed. Raw-byte wire issuance supports open/renew without resealing credentials on renew. Result receipts distinguish transient negotiation from a final outcome, retain immutable accepted identity/outcome and refuse divergent replacement. An ignored answer cannot reserve a receipt and poison a later valid reply. Audit failure atomically rolls back result/verification and permits the exact retry.

Stop, withdrawal, failure, revocation and expiry erase private signalling/material and create a durable close receipt in the existing CctvRequestReceipt owner. Pending closes are delivered before new live work and retried with the same peer/request/generation until a correlated stopped/expired/revoked acknowledgement commits. Failed replies cannot be counted as confirmation. A lost acknowledgement or audit failure retains pending shutdown. The native worker closes an existing peer even if its centrally issued renewal was never delivered, and retains a bounded tombstone for Close before Open so a late signed Open cannot resurrect the peer. Process receipts remain bounded without eviction. Governed lease release runs after the domain commit; unavailable revocation remains pending and the existing 15-second containment retry records its actual contained outcome.

Live and collector JSON endpoints now enforce closed original JSON bodies and private no-store errors, including generic temporary unavailability for internal/audit exceptions. ## 12 October 2026 — SD-01 Monitoring maintenance clock and draft occurrence review

The existing Monitoring owner now interprets local maintenance inputs in the selected IANA timezone and stores the reviewed UTC instants. Saved UTC records reopen in that timezone instead of displaying a sliced UTC clock. A recurring schedule requires explicit stored repeated-hour and skipped-hour choices; a nonexistent clock is never silently moved to a later time. Series limits are bounded to a year, the end is exclusive, intervals are clipped at that end, and unsafe overlapping recurring durations are rejected. Existing specificity, canonical Site ownership and technical failure suppression remain in MaintenanceEvaluator. A pure ZonedLocalTime helper and MaintenanceSchedule extend that owner; they do not introduce a second policy engine, provider execution or tenant scope.

The new private/no-store maintenance preview checks the fresh current actor, permission, approved Site and canonical device/monitor scope. It validates the entire bounded schedule and returns at most ten occurrences, explicit skips, selected clock rules and seconds precision. Store/update/cancel recheck current authority independently; preview is read-only and confers no write authority. Model and authoring values retain the two clock choices on a partial versioned edit. The additive migration defaults old rows to require review at ambiguous/missing clocks and refuses a downgrade while reviewed nondefault choices exist. Only isolated test migration occurred; deployment migration and legacy schedule review are still required.

The existing shared wizard now separates Plan and Review. It displays local times and their actual UTC offsets, retains unchanged stored seconds and selected repeated-hour offsets on edit, clears private drafts on actor/scope replacement, aborts or ignores stale previews, exposes all server validation errors, and verifies the saved record identity/version before clearing the draft. An unconfirmed result holds the current dialog and disables another save there. This is not a durable create identity or a canonical lost-response recovery operation; closing/reopening or refreshing is not an immutable receipt. That remaining contract must be implemented in the Monitoring owner.

## 12 October 2026 — SD-01 maintenance immutable command receipts and recovery

Maintenance create/edit/cancel now require a current actor and UUID v4 request identity on the public API. The existing Monitoring authoring owner is wrapped by one atomic command receipt: closed canonical input fingerprint, global UUID uniqueness, original actor/action/target binding, attributed before/after configuration and canonical scope snapshots, committed result/time and strict audit. Exact authorised replay resolves before stale-version/lifecycle rejection. The original committed window result is separate from the current window version/state, so recovery does not describe a later-cancelled window as currently active. The receipt model denies mutation/deletion after commit; the additive migration refuses a downgrade that would erase saved bindings. This remains configuration authoring, with no provider execution, permission grant or second policy engine.

MaintenancePolicyAccess reuses current callback-bounded authorization reads and the canonical Security & Devices access/Site resolver. Preview, mutation, replay and readback verify the current approved internal actor, permission, approved Site and device/monitor binding. The receipt pins the monitor's device and checks captured before/after scopes and the current window; changed source binding, lost permission or lost Site grant cannot return an old success under new authority. The private/no-store GET recovery route reads the current primary and never creates a request. The timing preview remains read-only and excludes command identity.

The existing Plan/Review and lifecycle dialogs now submit one frozen canonical body/key, verify the closed original receipt and expose Check saved request/Retry same request. The browser retains only an opaque identity, fingerprint, versions and numeric scope binding in session storage; no draft name, reason or dates are persisted. A reload offers readback, without a body to resend. An unconfirmed request blocks another maintenance mutation and freezes its displayed draft. Actor/context replacement aborts or ignores late replies, drops the private in-memory body and cannot clear a newer actor's marker. Current result messaging distinguishes committed version from current lifecycle state.

A read-only review from another chat identified a valid-input recovery defect: active Laravel TrimStrings and empty-to-null hygiene changed padded text before the server fingerprint, while the browser hashed raw text. Local framework source confirmed it. MaintenanceCommandInput now applies the same canonical hygiene for direct owner callers; the browser canonicalizes before fingerprinting and sending, using the installed Str::trim invisible character set plus Unicode whitespace and empty-to-null values. Meaningful interior whitespace is preserved. Receipt comparison remains strict and global middleware is unchanged. No message was sent back to that chat.
